## What Will You Learn

Week 1

### Mapping Business Outcomes to Product Outcomes

* Systems thinking
* Understanding Business Models
* Structured thinking with KPI trees
* Actors, Actions & Entities

Case Studies on

![Swiggy](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/swiggy_case_study_logo_239023.svg)

![YouTube](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/youtube_case_study_icont_389343.svg)

Week 2

### Product Discovery & User Research

* Impact Mapping & Opportunity sizing
* User Segmentation
* User Research Techniques
* Journey Mapping & Personas

Case Studies on

![Amazon](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/amazon_case_study_icong.svg)

![BeReal](https://assets.nextleap.app/image/eyJidWNrZXQiOiJuZXh0bGVhcC1zdGF0aWMtYXNzZXRzIiwia2V5IjoiaW1hZ2VzL2JlcmVhbC1jYXNlLXN0dWR5LTQ4OTYucG5nIiwiZWRpdHMiOnsicmVzaXplIjp7IndpZHRoIjo3MSwiaGVpZ2h0IjoxNiwiZml0IjoiY292ZXIiLCJ3aXRob3V0RW5sYXJnZW1lbnQiOnRydWV9LCJyb3RhdGUiOm51bGx9fQ==)

Week 3

### Problem Framing & Ideation

* Problem Framing Canvas
* Ideation & Product Risks
* Outcome-Opportunity-Solution tree
* User Flows & Wireframing

Product Manager

These past eight weeks have been an incredible whirlwind of learning, growth, and hands-on experience in the dynamic world of product management. From diving deep into market research and user-centric design to honing my communication and leadership skills, this program has equipped me with a robust toolkit to thrive in the ever-evolving tech industry.

## Frequently Asked Questions

* ### What is the NextLeap Product Manager Fellowship?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-remove-bca5f1fb-e4c1-4e78-a874-b74730361f09.svg)

  Our Product Manager Fellowship is a 16 week online course that helps learners transition into a career in product management. The entire fellowship is taught online via live sessions on Zoom. Our instructors and mentors are product management executives at top technology companies and they will not only teach you the fundamentals of product management, but also help you with case studies, doubt resolution and feedback.
* ### Who is eligible to apply for the NextLeap Product Manager Fellowship?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  This fellowship is open to anyone trying to break into a new role and there is no requirement for minimum experience, CGPA or degree / specialization.
* ### Are there any costs associated with the NextLeap Product Manager Fellowship?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  Apart from the enrollment fees there are no other hidden costs associated with the fellowship.
* ### What if I miss a live session?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  Recordings will be made available for all of the live sessions with instructors. Further, please note that we've designed our sessions to be participatory and interactive, and recommend that you attend all to make the most of them.
* ### What are the benefits of being a Fellow in the NextLeap Product Manager Fellowship program?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  As part of this fellowship you'll get (i) the opportunity to learn from instructors and mentors who are industry experts, (ii) to work on real world problem statements, (iii) comprehensive interview preparation with peers, alumni and hiring managers, and (iv) 1 year placement support.
* ### I'm a pre-final year student. Am I eligible to apply?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  Yes, you can apply for the program.
* ### What is the graduation project?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  At the end of the fellowship we will release a problem statement for you to work. Your submission to this problem statement is called the graduation project.
* ### Can I re-submit my graduation project?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  We will release a new problem statement at the start of every month. Once you have completed the fellowship, you will get 3 attempts in the next 3 consecutive months (after the fellowship) to submit your graduation project.
* ### What's the problem statement that I'll be required to work on for the graduation project?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  A problem statement will be released at the start of the first month after you have completed the fellowship. Thereafter, you'll have a time period of ~1 month to submit your graduation project.
* ### What are the key responsibilities of a Fellow during the program?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  Product Manager Fellowship requires a time commitment of 12-14 hours per week. As a fellow enrolled in this program, we will expect you to attend weekly live sessions with instructors and mentors, which are held on Saturdays. Aside from the same, you will be required to go through weekly learning material on the NextLeap website and make your submission for the weekly milestone, which is a real world problem statement on the topic being taught during the week. Upon completion of 8 weeks of the fellowship, you will get 3 monthly attempts at submitting the graduation project. All fellows who score more than 70% in the graduation project submission will be declared top fellows and will get the access to placement support for 1 year.
* ### What are the selection criteria for the NextLeap Product Fellowship?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  There's no selection criteria to enroll for our Product Manager Fellowship. However as part of this fellowship you will get 3 attempts to submit the graduation project and all fellows who score more than 70% in at least one of their attempts will be declared a top fellow. Thereafter, all top fellows will get 1 year placement support from NextLeap.
* ### Who all will be eligible to get access to career services?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  You need to submit your graduation project and score more than 70% in your project to get access to our career services.
* ### What’s included in the career services?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  As part of our career services, we’ll make your profile available to our partner hiring companies. Depending on their requirement and selection criteria they will reach out to you for interviews. Learners from our previous cohorts have gotten hired at companies such as OLA, PhonePe, Slice, Cars24, Media.net, Swiggy and many more.
* ### If I don’t score more than 70%, will I still get a completion certificate?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  Completion certificate will be given to every learner who completes all the weekly milestones and submits the graduation project.
* ### What kind of support and resources will Fellows have access to during the program?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  As part of your weekly live sessions with instructors you'll get to learn from industry experts. We also map each learner to a mentor who will help you every week with case study breakdowns and doubt resolution. Further, every week you'll gain access to learning material on the topic of the week and access to a real world problem statement as part of your weekly milestone.
* ### How many Fellowships are offered each year?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  A new cohort of the Product Manager Fellowship starts every month.
* ### Will the interviews be for internships or full time roles?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  Our hiring partners hire for both full time roles and internships (3-6 months). Typically, 70% of the hiring is for full time roles and 30% for internships.
* ### Is work experience also a criterion to get selected for interviews?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  No, not all! No prior experience is required. You need to complete your weekly milestones and score 70% on your graduation project to be eligible to appear for interviews.
* ### Which companies are hiring?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  There have been 500+ hiring partners so far. A lot of companies have already signed up for the new cohorts in each domain and the final list of the hiring partners will be shared upon commencement of the respective Fellowships.
* ### If I am unable to attend the program or need to pull out at the last minute, will I get a refund?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  Refund requests can be raised only before the 28th of the month preceding the Fellowship start date. If the reason is found genuine, we'll refund 100% of the paid amount. Any requests raised after the aforementioned timeline will not be processed. The decision on refund requests will be at the discretion of the NextLeap team.
* ### Can I get this reimbursed from my company?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  We provide a certificate of completion and an invoice as proof of your participation in this course. Our learners have found these documents to be sufficient to claim a reimbursement from their employers.
* ### How long is the NextLeap Product Manager Fellowship program?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  Our Product Manager Fellowship is an 16 week online course. After the completion of 16 weeks, you will get 3 attempts (1 per month) to submit the graduation project.
* ### What is the application process for the NextLeap Product Manager Fellowship?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  There's no application process for enrolling in our Product Manager Fellowship. You can simply click on Enroll Now and make the payment to enrol for the fellowship.
* ### Is NextLeap Product Management Fellowship worth it?

  ![expand](https://assets.nextleap.app/images/accordian-add-db9d73dd-301d-4686-89e7-65fd05f7af21.svg)

  As it goes, the proof is in the pudding. Check out the hundreds of success stories (https://nextleap.app/reviews) of our Fellows who have successfully transitioned into product manager roles post this product management course. So, if you are looking to transition into product roles and are willing to devote the required time and effort to pick up the skills, you have come to the right place.
